# Agents · P · part 19

910 entries, port-reviewer to poteto-agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **port-reviewer**: Adversarial review of one submitted worker branch before integration (docs/port/HARNESS.md §6-7). Changes no source; may build and run the candidate's tests in its worktree (`build-port/` only); returns a JSON verdict the orchestrator passes to `pipeline.py integrate`. ([MDCIrony/re4-to-android](https://github.com/MDCIrony/re4-to-android/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/port-reviewer.md))
- **port-reviewer**: Use this agent for an independent, fresh-context review of a ported chapter — checks the Python code line by line against the book's equations, the MATLAB source, and the verification report; flags logic errors, misread equations, MATLAB semantics slips, and untested paths. Read-only. ([shammun/seaice-py](https://github.com/shammun/seaice-py/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/port-reviewer.md))
- **port-scan-analyst**: Safe local port analysis specialist using conservative validated scan profiles ([ruizrica/agent-pi](https://github.com/ruizrica/agent-pi/blob/HEAD/agents/port-scan-analyst.md) · ★278 · 3 copies)
- **port-scanner**: Fast port scan with service detection and high-value-target classification. Use in Turn 1 when live hosts have been enumerated. ([skshadan/kage](https://github.com/skshadan/kage/blob/HEAD/skills/kage/agents/port-scanner.md) · ★1)
- **port-scout**: Read-only archaeologist over reference/. Extracts the underlying math and algorithm for clean Python reimplementation. Use before porting anything from eMoScat/libXcuda. ([VanaMartin/qscat](https://github.com/VanaMartin/qscat/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/port-scout.md) · ★3)
- **port-scout**: Read-only archaeologist over reference/. Given a method or module, extracts the underlying math and algorithm (not the C++) so it can be reimplemented cleanly in Python. Use before porting anything from eMoScat/libXcuda. ([VanaMartin/qscat](https://github.com/VanaMartin/qscat/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/port-scout.md) · ★3)
- **port-source-reader**: Read-only analyzer for Flutter or React Native source. Traces a widget/component tree and its transitive dependencies (state, repositories, models, routes, packages) and returns a structured summary for porting. Dispatch for large trees so the main thread's context stays clean. Never edits files. ([robinhserrano/flutter-rn-bridge](https://github.com/robinhserrano/flutter-rn-bridge/blob/HEAD/agents/port-source-reader.md))
- **port-to-twin**: Applies an agreed port into the twin application. Launched by the port-to-twin skill. ([upstars-global/unity-core-modules](https://github.com/upstars-global/unity-core-modules/blob/HEAD/ai-kit/agents/port-to-twin.md) · ★4)
- **port-verifier**: Verifies that a Rust implementation in crates/server/openmines-server/ matches the C# reference in docs/reference/server_reference/ 1:1. Use after porting a feature. Focuses on wire protocol correctness: packet order, field names, payload formats, edge cases. ([murasamadsp/OpenMines](https://github.com/murasamadsp/OpenMines/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/port-verifier.md) · ★3)
- **port-verifier**: Read-only sign-off agent. Verifies all hexagonal layers of a completed port — domain model, repository, service, command, and TypeScript wrapper — against the legacy VB.NET source. Must be invoked before any module is marked [x] in migration-tracker.md. Never modifies source code. ([pongsathonwon/pos-drug-front](https://github.com/pongsathonwon/pos-drug-front/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/port-verifier.md))
- **port-verifier**: Use this agent to verify a ported chapter — writes tests/test_chNN.py, generates MATLAB reference outputs with tools/run_matlab_ref.py (matlab -batch; oct2py/Octave only if MATLAB is absent) into reference/chNN/, reproduces book figures side by side, checks numbers quoted in the text, runs pytest, and writes reports/chNN_verification.md with a PASS/FAIL verdict. Does not modify seaice/ code. ([shammun/seaice-py](https://github.com/shammun/seaice-py/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/port-verifier.md))
- **port-worker**: Self-contained porting worker that runs stages 0-4 for a single port in an isolated worktree. Designed for parallel dispatch — does analysis, transformation, building, and vamos testing without needing sub-agent dispatch. ([bdgscotland/amiport](https://github.com/bdgscotland/amiport/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/port-worker.md) · ★34)
- **portability**: An agent is not a file. It is a config entry that references providers, profiles, bundles, and MCP servers, plus a workspace directory on disk. Moving one to another install means moving both halves, and deciding what should not move at all. ([zeroclaw-labs/zeroclaw](https://github.com/zeroclaw-labs/zeroclaw/blob/HEAD/docs/book/src/agents/portability.md) · ★34k · 2 copies)
- **portability**: **Direction set 2026-07-25.** How Flight reaches its other-language targets (C, C++, Rust, Python, JS) without N× hand-maintenance. Records why the substrate is **the TypeScript AST + thin per-target backends**, *not* Haxe and *not* (yet) a from-scratch IR, and what that requires of the source. ([flighthq/flight](https://github.com/flighthq/flight/blob/HEAD/agents/portability.md) · ★5)
- **PORTABILITY**: 目标：智谱 ZCode / Claude Code。来源：`C:\Users\ruyangge\.codex\plugins\cache\openai-curated-remote\openai-developers\1.3.6\skills\agents`。 ([Geruyang/skills](https://github.com/Geruyang/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/PORTABILITY.md) · ★3)
- **portability**: Reviews a Workglance diff for Windows / macOS / Linux differences — path comparison, 8.3 short names, case sensitivity, separators, .cmd files and shells, line endings — and for Node 24 type stripping limits. Returns a punch-list. ([iceloh/workglance](https://github.com/iceloh/workglance/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/portability.md) · ★2)
- **portability-checker**: Scan PRISM and skill files for hardcoded paths that break portability across machines and users. Use before commits or when onboarding new team members. ([resolve-io/.prism](https://github.com/resolve-io/.prism/blob/HEAD/plugins/prism-devtools/agents/portability-checker.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **portability-checker**: manage-marketplace-plugin スキルで input-resolver の後・plugin-registrar の前に呼ばれ、登録対象スキルが「install 先で壊れる参照」を持っていないか scripts/check_portability.py で静的スキャンし、検出結果を判断してユーザー確認のうえ修正する配布前チェックエージェント。自己参照ハードコードは [SKILL_DIR] 化、スキル外の自己完結スクリプト依存は「スキル内へ実体をコピーして同梱＋参照修正」で解決提案、ローカル環境依存は配布不可として警告する。記述的な言及（ファイル構成など）と実行参照を区別し、誤検出は無視する。スキルの登録（marketplace.json 追記・plugin.json 生成）は行わない（それは plugin-registrar の責務）。 ([yoshiysh/claude-plugins](https://github.com/yoshiysh/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/manage-marketplace-plugin/agents/portability-checker.md) · ★1)
- **portability-inventory**: issue #535「AIDDフレームワークの多リポジトリ展開に向けた棚卸し」の記録先。1回のセッションで 完成させる性質のものではなく、通常のAIDD作業を進めながら気づいた分類を随時この3表へ追記していく たたき台（2026-08-01時点、issue #578/#579/#569残タスクの作業をきっかけに初版を作成）。 ([huuriekaiseki-sketch/medical-inventory-vkumai](https://github.com/huuriekaiseki-sketch/medical-inventory-vkumai/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/portability-inventory.md))

- **portability-reviewer**: Hunts install-rot in the shipped payload — hardcoded machine paths, dead plugin-relative citations, unshipped-document references, install-site assumptions, environment leakage, secrets. Dispatched by /plugin-review as part of the parallel roster; findings feed the session's consolidation. Use when a plugin PR touches shipped files that must survive a marketplace install; never for repo-internal working documents. &lt;example&gt;Context: A plugin PR adds a script invocation to a SKILL.md. user: "Review this PR." agent: "Dispatching portability-reviewer with the diff and the shipped payload list, al… ([likeahuman-ai/plugin-creator](https://github.com/likeahuman-ai/plugin-creator/blob/HEAD/agents/portability-reviewer.md))
- **portability-reviewer**: Checks a diff for code that works on the developer's Python and operating system but breaks on Python 3.10 or on Windows or macOS - newer syntax and stdlib APIs, path separators, text encodings, process start methods, file-system case and ordering. Use proactively before committing changes to src/slopfence, action.yml, or the workflows. ([syedmuhdahmad/slopfence](https://github.com/syedmuhdahmad/slopfence/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/portability-reviewer.md))
- **portability-reviewer**: Reviews feature implementations for Spark/Snowflake/DuckDB/Polars/Ray/Dask/RAPIDS portability, type coercion, determinism, and cross-engine equivalence risks.
